# Verifier coverage of the endpoints changed since the last release (2026-10-21)

Release gate (Andrew, 2026-10-21): every endpoint that is new or changed since the last published release is reached by a verifier lane and by a fault-injection arm the verifier or a check provably catches. Range `fc8d425fb..81afc48b9`. Generated by `tools/verify_coverage_audit.py` (static: the export walk of `tools/public_runtime_inventory.py`, `tools/lane_select.py`'s Mojo definition graph, and a scoped name closure from each lane's own code); the rows marked by hand are the `ANNOTATIONS` table in that file, each checked in the source.

Regenerate:

```sh
python3 tools/verify_coverage_audit.py --base fc8d425fb --json <audit.json> --markdown docs/plans/VERIFY_COVERAGE_20261021.md
```
